## core.118.hint

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acore.118.hint&lang=en)

### 中文

收集£energy£['concept_energy']需要在£interface£['concept_colony_view']的$colony_view_tab_planet_summary$选项卡建造£district£['concept_generator_districts']。\n\n或者可以在蕴含能量的['concept_celestial_objects']建造£mining_station£['concept_mining_stations']、在£market£['concept_market']或向其他['concept_empires']购买£energy£§H$concept_energy$§!。$focus_other$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:115`

### English

To gather £energy£ ['concept_energy'], build £district£ ['concept_generator_districts'] in the $colony_view_tab_planet_summary$ tab of the £interface£ ['concept_colony_view'].\n\nAlternatively, build £mining_station£ ['concept_mining_stations'] around energy-rich ['concept_celestial_objects'], buy £energy£ §H$concept_energy$§! on the £market£ ['concept_market'], or from other ['concept_empires'].$focus_other$

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:137`

§L消费品助力创新、提升文化、满足大众需求。具体是什么呢？奢侈品？高级工具？还是一顶漂亮的帽子？都是。”§!

### English

§L“在方舟之上，每条丝线、电路，乃至安逸之享都有两次生命：第一次在产生之时，第二次在维护之中。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:363`

### English

Use a £science_ship£ ['concept_science_ship'] to survey new £system£ ['concept_systems'] until alien life is found.

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:421`

## exploration.115.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aexploration.115.lore&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“搜寻外星生命不是在问有没有，而是问什么时候找到。当找到的时候又要问：我们真能认出来它们是什么东西吗？”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

§L'The search for alien life is not a question of if, but when. And when we find it, we must ask: will we recognize it for what it is?'§!

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:422`

## exploration.205.hint

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aexploration.205.hint&lang=en)

### 中文

指派有£scientist£['concept_scientist']指挥的£science_ship£['concept_science_ship']调查£system£['concept_systems']、研究£anomaly£['concept_anomalies']。也可以指派其作为['concept_governor']或作为['concept_council']。\n\n每项任务都可以提供宝贵的经验值，使其等级提升并发展出新['concept_leader_traits']。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

Assign a £science_ship£ ['concept_science_ship'] led by a £scientist£ ['concept_scientist'] to survey £system£ ['concept_systems'] and investigate £anomaly£ ['concept_anomalies']. Alternatively, assign them as a ['concept_governor'] or to the ['concept_council'].\n\nSuch duties provide valuable experience, allowing them to level up and develop new ['concept_leader_traits'].

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:453`

## exploration.425.hint

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aexploration.425.hint&lang=en)

### 中文

要新建一艘§H$science_arkship_name$§!，选择一艘现有的£arkship£ ['concept_arkship']并点击§H建造巨型结构§!。在其中选择方舟舰规划类型进行建造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

To build a new §H$science_arkship_name$§!, select an existing £arkship£ ['concept_arkship'] and click §HBuild Megastructure§!. From there, select the Arkship Class to build.

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:589`

## exploration.425.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aexploration.425.lore&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“把图书馆建造在星球上只会分崩离析，而我们建造在飞船上则可一日万里。在我们的好奇心耗尽之前，宇宙的奥秘将会被悉数揭开。”—— 李恩·托拉迪教授的方舟理学院毕业典礼演讲§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

§L'They built libraries on worlds that crumbled. We built ours on an engine that never stops. The universe will run out of secrets before we run out of curiosity.' - Professor Lienn Toradi, commencement speech at the Arkship Academy of Sciences§!

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:590`

## exploration.425.req

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aexploration.425.req&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

## conquest.111.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.111.lore&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“将一个生命体塑造成一件武器……有人称之为进步，有人称之为亵渎。但是历史只会记住谁活了下来。”——《基因学与战争艺术》哈斯克·祖蒙博士§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:577`

### English

§L'To take a living organism and mold it into a weapon... Some call it progress. Others, blasphemy. History will only remember who survived.' - Dr. Hask'Zumon, Genetics and the Art of War§!

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:666`

## conquest.205.hint

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.205.hint&lang=en)

### 中文

部署一支由£commander£['concept_commander']指挥的['concept_fleet']，进入战场战斗。也可以指派其作为['concept_governor']或作为['concept_council']。\n\n每项任务都可以提供宝贵的经验值，使其等级提升并发展出新['concept_leader_traits']。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:618`

### English

Deploy a ['concept_fleet'], led by a £commander£ ['concept_commander'], to engage in battle. Alternatively, assign them as a ['concept_governor'] or to the ['concept_council'].\n\nSuch duties provide valuable experience, allowing them to level up and develop new ['concept_leader_traits'].

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:713`

## conquest.205.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.205.lore&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“菜鸟学习战斗的技巧。老道的指挥官学习战斗的时机。”——《新太空战争学说》§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:619`

### English

§L'Rookies learn how to fight. Experienced Commanders learn when to fight.' - New Doctrines in Space Warfare§!

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:714`

## conquest.212.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.212.lore&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“织蛛可不只是战舰，它们是战力倍增器，是决定你的舰队是一盘散沙还是有机整体的关键。”——《突触战争学说》第四卷§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:640`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.305.hint&lang=en)

### 中文

在£interface£['concept_contacts_view']的$contacts_view_tab_contacts$选项卡，向另一个['concept_empire']发起['concept_war']。建造并指挥一支强大的£fleet_size£['concept_fleet']，与敌军交战，占领关键£planet£['concept_colonies']直到获得胜利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:667`

### English

In the $contacts_view_tab_contacts$ tab of the £interface£ ['concept_contacts_view'], declare a ['concept_war'] with another ['concept_empire']. Build and command a strong £fleet_size£ ['concept_fleet'], engage enemy forces, and occupy key £planet£ ['concept_colonies'] until victory is secured.

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:769`

## conquest.305.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.305.lore&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“胜利不是结束，而是负担的转移。愚妄者以征服为乐，智者早就开始了从长计议。”——《刀锋之缘》华瓦上师§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:668`

### English

§L'Victory is not the end, but the shifting of burdens. The foolish revel in conquest; the wise turn to what follows.' - Master Huava, The Edge of the Blade'§!

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:770`

## conquest.320.hint

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.320.hint&lang=en)

### 中文

将一支['concept_fleet']部署在敌对£colony£['concept_colony']的上空便会开始§H轨道轰炸§!。不使用['concept_armies']加以入侵而是让§H$concept_fleet$§!轰炸§H$concept_colony$§!直到其投降为止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:688`

### English

Place a ['concept_fleet'] in orbit of a hostile £colony£ ['concept_colony'], which starts §HOrbital Bombardment§!. Instead of using ['concept_armies'] to invade the planet, let the §H$concept_fleet$§! bombard the §H$concept_colony$§! until it surrenders.

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:793`

## conquest.320.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.320.lore&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“攻城不是力量的比拼，而是韧性的较量。行星撑住了，舰队就会陷入一片火海；舰队撑住了，行星就不得不卑躬屈膝。”——《持久战》莱恩托克·沙尔·帕拉特§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:689`

### English

§L'A siege is not a contest of strength, but of endurance. The planet holds, and the fleet burns. The fleet holds, the planet kneels.' - Rhentok shar Platt, A Protracted War§!

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:794`

## conquest.325.hint

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.325.hint&lang=en)

### 中文

在£interface£['concept_claims_view']，向另一['concept_empire']的起始§H§!£colony£['concept_colonies']放置['concept_claims']。在£interface£['concept_contacts_view']的$contacts_view_tab_contacts$选项卡宣布§H宣称§!['concept_war']。让['concept_armies']登陆入侵该§H母星§!并结束战争。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:695`

### English

In the £interface£ ['concept_claims_view'], put ['concept_claims'] on the starting §HHomeworld§! £colony£ ['concept_colonies'] of another ['concept_empire']. In the $contacts_view_tab_contacts$ tab of the £interface£ ['concept_contacts_view'], declare a ['concept_war'] of §HClaim§!. Land ['concept_armies'] on the §HHomeworld§! to invade them and finish the war.

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:801`

### 中文

§L“身经百战的指挥官不是从训练和纸上谈兵里来的。他们要从无情的虚空中淬炼而出。”§!

## conquest.430.hint

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.430.hint&lang=en)

### 中文

要建造新的§H$military_arkship_name$§!，请选择一艘现有的£arkship£ ['concept_arkship']，然后点击§H建造巨型结构§!。随后，选择要建造的方舟舰级别。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:751`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:865`

## conquest.430.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.430.lore&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“让他们去测绘群星，让他们去编录虚空。而我们要做的，就是让他们在埋头于此之时，也不得不时刻提防身后。”——战略家尤拉·维恩，《论权力的目的》§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:752`

### English

§L'Let them map the stars. Let them catalogue the void. We will be the reason they look over their shoulder while they do it.' - Strategos Yura Venn, On the Purpose of Power§!

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:866`

## conquest.505.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.505.lore&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“附庸不是盟友，而是一种资源，需要加以栽培、控制，若有需要，还得矫正。”——威尔·乌格拉姆总理§!

## conquest.510.hint

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.510.hint&lang=en)

### 中文

在£interface£['concept_claims_view']，向另一个['concept_empire']的£colony£['concept_colonies']放置['concept_claims']。在£interface£['concept_contacts_view']的$contacts_view_tab_contacts$选项卡，宣布§H宣称§!['concept_war']。让['concept_armies']登陆入侵该星球并结束战争。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:772`

### English

In the £interface£ ['concept_claims_view'], put ['concept_claims'] on the £colony£ ['concept_colonies'] of another ['concept_empire']. In the $contacts_view_tab_contacts$ tab of the £interface£ ['concept_contacts_view'], declare a ['concept_war'] of §HClaim§!. Land ['concept_armies'] on the worlds to invade them and finish the war.

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:889`

## conquest.510.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.510.lore&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“征服不是毁灭性的，而是一种重定向。最明智的国家无需增长——只需吸纳即可。”§!

### English

§L'Conquest is not destruction; it is redirection. The wisest nations do not grow - they absorb.'§!

## conquest.540.hint

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.540.hint&lang=en)

### 中文

要在£arkship£ ['concept_arkship']上装备§H轴基挂载§!，请前往完全升级的§H方舟舰§!上的£interface£ ['concept_colony_view']的§H$ARKSHIP_CONTROLS$§!选项卡。点击§H轴基挂载槽位§!，然后从列表中选择§H毁灭者挂载§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:814`

### English

To equip a §HSpinal Mount§! on an £arkship£ ['concept_arkship'], go to the §H$ARKSHIP_CONTROLS$§! tab of the £interface£ ['concept_colony_view'] on a fully upgraded §HArkship§!. Click on the §HSpinal Mount Slot§! and select the §HDevastator Mount§! from the list.

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:937`

## conquest.540.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.540.lore&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“命令下达时，每一层甲板的灯光都会暗下。黑暗中，数百万人屏住呼吸；随后，虚空反而亮起。船员们称之为‘长瞬’。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:815`

### English

§L'The lights dim across every deck when the order is given. Millions hold their breath in the dark, and then the void lights up instead. The crew calls it the Long Blink.'§!

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:938`

## conquest.540.req

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconquest.540.req&lang=en)

派遣一艘£science_ship£['concept_science_ship']调查新£system£['concept_systems']直至发起§H第一次接触§!流程。指派一位['concept_envoy']来领导这项工作直到得到结果。

## development.100.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adevelopment.100.lore&lang=en)

§L“两个文明相遇的那一刻，历史的轨迹便开始变动。这条路会通向和平或是战争，或许就在一个词语之间。”——特使达特利·尤康纳§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:825`

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:950`

## development.215.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adevelopment.215.lore&lang=en)

## development.235.hint

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adevelopment.235.hint&lang=en)

### 中文

在£interface£ ['concept_galaxy_map']上接受一份§H$arkship_harvest_resources$§!合约，然后派遣['concept_logistic_ships']执行§H$arkship_harvest_resources$§!舰队指令。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:922`

### English

## development.235.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adevelopment.235.lore&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“开采期间，提取舱热得发烫；每一道舱壁上都凝满水汽，船员轮班作业，只为让处理机永不停歇。我们不留下岩石，只留下名声。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:923`

### English

§L'The extraction bay runs hot during a harvest; condensation on every bulkhead, crews working in shifts so the processors never cool down. We don't keep the rock, just the reputation.'§!

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:1062`

## development.305.hint

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adevelopment.305.hint&lang=en)

### 中文

在研究£engineering£§H$tech_orbital_arc_furnace$§!科技后，在至少拥有15颗£planet£['concept_planets']的£system£['concept_system']找到一颗合适的§H熔融星球§!并派遣一艘£construction_ship£['concept_construction_ship']建造§H$tech_orbital_arc_furnace$§!£megastructure£['concept_megastructure']。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:936`

### English

After researching the £engineering£ §H$tech_orbital_arc_furnace$§! technology, find a §HMolten World§! in a £system£ ['concept_system'] with at least 15 £planet£ ['concept_planets'] and use a £construction_ship£ ['concept_construction_ship'] to build the §H$tech_orbital_arc_furnace$§! £megastructure£ ['concept_megastructure'].

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:1077`

## development.305.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adevelopment.305.lore&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“恒星在地平线上升起，刹那间整座结构都燃烧着金色的光。数千公里长的脚手架和钢材……由我们建成。我们让这颗星球成为了自己的熔炉。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:937`

### English

§L'The sun crests the horizon, and for a moment, the whole structure burns gold. Thousands of kilometers of scaffolding and steel... We built this. We made this planet our forge.'§!

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:1078`

§L“让这项协议成为我们决心的证明：我们选择团结而非分裂，选择对话而非战争，坚信星系的共同命运。”——《星海共同体序言》§!

要在£arkship£ ['concept_arkship'] 上装备一具§H轴基挂载§!，请前往完全升级的§H方舟舰§!的 £interface£ ['concept_colony_view'] 中的§H$ARKSHIP_CONTROLS$§!选项卡。点击§H轴基挂载槽位§!，并从列表中选择§H主力舰船坞§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:1070`

### English

To equip a §HSpinal Mount§! on an £arkship£ ['concept_arkship'], go to the §H$ARKSHIP_CONTROLS$§! tab of the £interface£ ['concept_colony_view'] on a fully upgraded §HArkship§!. Click on the §HSpinal Mount Slot§! and select the §HCapital Shipyard§! from the list.

Source: `localisation/english/focus_l_english.yml:1230`

## development.525.lore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adevelopment.525.lore&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“每一次航向修正、每一条轨道、每一次漂移：船坞都一一应对。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/focus_l_simp_chinese.yml:1071`

### English
